# ===================== PHASE 3: SEALED EVALUATION =====================
# Frozen SEALED window parameters are reused exactly from af_phase0.py:
# SEALED day = 2017-11-08; DAY_START_ROW = 68_941_878; SKIP_MARGIN = 6_000_000.
# No LORD++ re-simulation (trial-level FDR state is not embedded). Decoys are descriptive post hoc checks.

PHASE3_SEEDS = (100, 101, 102)
SEALED_CEILING = EMBEDDED_SPEC_FREEZE["results"]["SEALED"]["ceiling"]
SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN = EMBEDDED_SPEC_FREEZE["results"]["SEALED"]["baseline"]
DEV_CEILING = F["dev_ceiling"]
PHASE3_BASE_TOL = 0.0002
EXPECTED_INPUTS_SHA256 = "afd7964928b9ac98249402db19a507d1b39fb4c3142bfd347976626dae8a420b"
P3_ARMS = ["L-FDR", "L-FDR-14B", "L-FDR-3B", "L-naive", "R", "R-naive"]
P3_NAIVE_ARMS = ("L-naive", "R-naive")
P3_EXPECTED_DECOYS = {"R": 6, "R-naive": 3, "L-naive": 0, "L-FDR": 6, "L-FDR-3B": 1, "L-FDR-14B": 13}

RUN_COLS = ["arm", "run", "final_set_hash", "n_accepted_real", "n_decoys_tested", "n_decoys_accepted", "decoy_fpr",
            "sealed_baseline", "sealed_final_auc", "sealed_gap_closed_pct", "dev_baseline", "dev_final_auc",
            "dev_gap_closed_pct", "optimism_gap", "model", "minutes", "tokens_prompt", "tokens_completion",
            "n_proposals", "n_full_tests", "decoys_proposed", "decoys_screened", "dups"]
DECOY_COLS = ["arm", "run", "decoy_index", "decoy_idx", "spec", "repermute_seed", "sealed_stack_auc",
              "sealed_stack_decoy_auc", "delta_auc", "naive_pass", "materiality_pass"]
ARM_COLS = ["arm", "runs", "n_effective_outcomes", "mean_gap_closed", "median_gap_closed", "min_gap_closed",
            "max_gap_closed", "mean_final_auc", "mean_dev_gap_closed", "mean_optimism_gap", "mean_real_accepted",
            "decoys_tested", "decoys_accepted", "decoy_fpr"]


def p3_now():
    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())


def p3_atomic_text(path, text):
    tmp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    with open(tmp, "w") as f:
        f.write(text)
        f.flush()
        os.fsync(f.fileno())
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def phase3_write_json(path, obj):
    p3_atomic_text(path, json.dumps(obj, indent=2, default=float))


def p3_hash(obj) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(obj, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()


def p3_spec(item: dict) -> dict:
    """Embedded accepted records ARE specs (op/keys/...); tolerate a {'spec': ...} wrapper too."""
    return item["spec"] if "spec" in item and "op" not in item else item


def p3_guard(measured: float, frozen: float, tol: float) -> None:
    if abs(measured - frozen) > tol:
        raise RuntimeError(f"SEALED baseline mismatch: measured={measured:.7f}, frozen={frozen:.7f}, tol={tol}. Aborting.")


def p3_nan(x):
    return float("nan") if x is None else x


class P3Evaluator:
    """One Lab per SEALED window; raw baseline measured once; features and final stacks cached by hash."""

    def __init__(self, lab: Lab):
        self.lab = lab
        lab.reset()
        self._cols = {}
        self._stacks = {}
        raw = self._fits([])
        self.baseline = [dict(seed=sd, auc=float(f["auc"]), iters=f["iters"]) for sd, f in zip(PHASE3_SEEDS, raw)]
        self.baseline_mean = float(np.mean([f["auc"] for f in self.baseline]))

    def _fits(self, extras):
        return [self.lab.full.fit(extras, s) for s in PHASE3_SEEDS]

    def col(self, spec):
        k = p3_hash(spec)
        if k not in self._cols:
            self._cols[k] = self.lab.full.col(self.lab.full_cols(spec, False, 0))
        return self._cols[k]

    def stack(self, specs):
        h = p3_hash(specs)
        if h not in self._stacks:
            fits = self._fits([self.col(s) for s in specs])
            self._stacks[h] = dict(hash=h, fits=[dict(seed=s, auc=float(f["auc"]), iters=f["iters"])
                                                 for s, f in zip(PHASE3_SEEDS, fits)],
                                   mean=float(np.mean([f["auc"] for f in fits])))
        return self._stacks[h]

    def decoy(self, specs, decoy_spec, dseed):
        base = self.stack(specs)
        extra = self.lab.full.col(self.lab.full_cols(decoy_spec, True, dseed))
        fits = self._fits([self.col(s) for s in specs] + [extra])
        aucs = [float(f["auc"]) for f in fits]
        per_seed = [a - b["auc"] for a, b in zip(aucs, base["fits"])]          # matched seeds 100/101/102
        return dict(stack_auc=base["mean"], stack_decoy_auc=float(np.mean(aucs)),
                    delta=float(np.mean(per_seed)), per_seed=per_seed)


P3_DECOY_SCHEDULE = {1: 10, 2: 15, 3: 10, 4: 16, 5: 9}     # decoys proposed per run number (same across arms)


def p3_fix_tokens(df):
    """Embedded token counters are cumulative across runs within an arm (strictly increasing) -> per-run deltas.
    Run 1 of each arm is taken as-is (assumes the counter started at 0; unverified)."""
    df = df.copy()
    df["tokens_cumulative_converted"] = False
    for col in ("tokens_prompt", "tokens_completion"):
        df[col + "_cumulative"] = df[col]
        for arm, g in df.groupby("arm"):
            g = g.sort_values("run")
            v = g[col + "_cumulative"].astype(float)
            if v.notna().all() and len(v) > 1 and (v.diff().dropna() > 0).all():
                df.loc[g.index, col] = v.diff().fillna(v.iloc[0])
                df.loc[g.index, "tokens_cumulative_converted"] = True
    return df


def p3_tokens(t):
    if isinstance(t, dict):
        return t.get("prompt"), t.get("completion")
    return None, None


def p3_execute(ev: P3Evaluator, runs: dict, work: Path, expected: bool, log_prefix=""):
    """Replay all runs; write per-run flushes, final CSV/JSON/MD outputs and the completion lock."""
    lock_path = work / "phase3_lock.json"
    run_rows, decoy_rows = [], []
    t_start = time.time()
    n_decoys_total = 0
    for run_index, (run_id, run) in enumerate(runs.items()):
        assert isinstance(run, dict), f"embedded run {run_id!r} is not a dictionary"
        arm, run_number = run["arm"], run["run"]
        tag = f"{arm}_{run_number}"
        specs = [p3_spec(it) for it in run["accepted"] if not it.get("is_decoy", False)]
        if expected:
            assert len(specs) == len(run["accepted"]), f"{tag}: embedded accepted list contains decoy records"
        print(f"{log_prefix}===== {tag}: evaluating {len(specs)} accepted real features =====", flush=True)
        res = ev.stack(specs)
        sbase = ev.baseline_mean
        sfinal = res["mean"]
        gap = 100.0 * (sfinal - sbase) / (SEALED_CEILING - sbase)

        decoys = []
        for decoy_index, d in enumerate(run.get("decoys", [])):
            dseed = 7_000_000 + 1000 * run_index + decoy_index
            r = ev.decoy(specs, d["spec"], dseed)
            n_decoys_total += 1
            drow = dict(arm=arm, run=run_number, decoy_index=decoy_index, decoy_idx=d.get("idx"),
                        spec=json.dumps(d["spec"], sort_keys=True), repermute_seed=dseed,
                        sealed_stack_auc=r["stack_auc"], sealed_stack_decoy_auc=r["stack_decoy_auc"],
                        delta_auc=r["delta"], naive_pass=bool(r["delta"] > 0.0),
                        materiality_pass=bool(r["delta"] >= F["materiality"]),
                        dev_accepted=d.get("dev_accepted"), per_seed_delta=json.dumps(r["per_seed"]))
            decoys.append(drow)
            decoy_rows.append(drow)

        # decoy "accepted" mirrors each arm's own gate minus the (not re-simulated) FDR state
        key = "naive_pass" if arm in P3_NAIVE_ARMS else "materiality_pass"
        n_acc = sum(int(d[key]) for d in decoys)
        dev_base, dev_final = run.get("dev_baseline"), run.get("dev_final_auc")
        dev_gap = (100.0 * (dev_final - dev_base) / (DEV_CEILING - dev_base)
                   if dev_base is not None and dev_final is not None else float("nan"))
        tp, tc = p3_tokens(run.get("tokens"))
        proposed, screened = run.get("decoys_proposed"), run.get("decoys_screened")
        counts_src = "embedded"
        if (proposed is None or proposed == 0 or proposed < len(decoys)) and run_number in P3_DECOY_SCHEDULE:
            # R and L-naive CSVs never logged these. The schedule is identical across arms per run number (verified on
            # R-naive, L-FDR, L-FDR-3B, L-FDR-14B), and screened = proposed - gate-tested in every embedded row.
            proposed = P3_DECOY_SCHEDULE[run_number]
            screened = proposed - len(decoys)
            counts_src = "derived_from_schedule"
        row = dict(arm=arm, run=run_number, final_set_hash=res["hash"], n_accepted_real=len(specs),
                   n_decoys_tested=len(decoys), n_decoys_accepted=n_acc,
                   decoy_fpr=(n_acc / len(decoys)) if decoys else float("nan"),
                   sealed_baseline=sbase, sealed_final_auc=sfinal, sealed_gap_closed_pct=gap,
                   dev_baseline=p3_nan(dev_base), dev_final_auc=p3_nan(dev_final), dev_gap_closed_pct=dev_gap,
                   optimism_gap=dev_gap - gap, model=run.get("model"), minutes=p3_nan(run.get("minutes")),
                   tokens_prompt=p3_nan(tp), tokens_completion=p3_nan(tc),
                   n_proposals=p3_nan(run.get("n_proposals")), n_full_tests=p3_nan(run.get("n_full_tests")),
                   decoys_proposed=p3_nan(proposed), decoys_screened=p3_nan(screened),
                   dups=p3_nan(run.get("n_dup_total")), decoy_counts_source=counts_src)
        run_rows.append(row)
        print(f"{log_prefix}{tag}: baseline={sbase:.5f} final={sfinal:.5f} gap_closed={gap:.1f}% "
              f"decoys(acc/tested)={n_acc}/{len(decoys)} | elapsed {(time.time() - t_start) / 60:.1f} min", flush=True)
        phase3_write_json(work / f"phase3_run_{tag}.json", dict(run=row, accepted_real_specs=specs,
                          base_fits=ev.baseline, final_fits=res["fits"], decoys=decoys))
        phase3_write_json(lock_path, {**json.loads(lock_path.read_text()), "runs_done": len(run_rows),
                                      "decoys_done": n_decoys_total, "last_run": tag})

    runs_df = p3_fix_tokens(pd.DataFrame(run_rows))
    runs_df = runs_df[RUN_COLS + [c for c in runs_df.columns if c not in RUN_COLS]]
    run_rows = runs_df.to_dict(orient="records")
    dec_df = pd.DataFrame(decoy_rows)
    for c in DECOY_COLS:
        if c not in dec_df.columns:
            dec_df[c] = pd.Series(dtype=object)
    dec_df = dec_df[DECOY_COLS + [c for c in dec_df.columns if c not in DECOY_COLS]]
    arms = []
    for arm in sorted(runs_df.arm.unique()):
        x = runs_df[runs_df.arm == arm]
        tested, acc = int(x.n_decoys_tested.sum()), int(x.n_decoys_accepted.sum())
        arms.append(dict(arm=arm, runs=len(x), n_effective_outcomes=x.final_set_hash.nunique(),
                         mean_gap_closed=x.sealed_gap_closed_pct.mean(), median_gap_closed=x.sealed_gap_closed_pct.median(),
                         min_gap_closed=x.sealed_gap_closed_pct.min(), max_gap_closed=x.sealed_gap_closed_pct.max(),
                         mean_final_auc=x.sealed_final_auc.mean(), mean_dev_gap_closed=x.dev_gap_closed_pct.mean(),
                         mean_optimism_gap=x.optimism_gap.mean(), mean_real_accepted=x.n_accepted_real.mean(),
                         decoys_tested=tested, decoys_accepted=acc,
                         decoy_fpr=(acc / tested) if tested else float("nan")))
    arms_df = pd.DataFrame(arms)[ARM_COLS]

    if expected:
        assert len(runs_df) == 30 and runs_df.arm.nunique() == 6
        assert n_decoys_total == 29, n_decoys_total
        got = {a: int(arms_df.loc[arms_df.arm == a, "decoys_tested"].iloc[0]) for a in P3_ARMS}
        assert got == P3_EXPECTED_DECOYS, got

    runs_df.to_csv(work / "phase3_runs.csv", index=False)
    phase3_write_json(work / "phase3_runs.json", run_rows)
    dec_df.to_csv(work / "phase3_decoys.csv", index=False)
    arms_df.to_csv(work / "phase3_arms.csv", index=False)
    md = ["| " + " | ".join(ARM_COLS) + " |", "|" + "---|" * len(ARM_COLS)]
    for r in arms_df.round(4).itertuples(index=False):
        md.append("| " + " | ".join(str(v) for v in r) + " |")
    p3_atomic_text(work / "phase3_arms.md", "\n".join(md) + "\n")
    phase3_write_json(work / "phase3_summary.json", dict(
        status="complete", sealed_baseline_frozen=SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN, sealed_baseline_measured=ev.baseline_mean,
        sealed_ceiling_frozen=SEALED_CEILING, dev_ceiling=DEV_CEILING, runs=run_rows,
        arms=arms_df.to_dict(orient="records")))
    p3_atomic_text(work / "PHASE3_METHOD_NOTES.md", P3_NOTES)
    phase3_write_json(lock_path, {**json.loads(lock_path.read_text()), "status": "complete",
                                  "completed_at_utc": p3_now(), "n_runs": len(run_rows), "n_arms": len(arms_df),
                                  "n_decoys_tested": n_decoys_total,
                                  "sealed_baseline_frozen": SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN,
                                  "sealed_ceiling_frozen": SEALED_CEILING})
    return runs_df, dec_df, arms_df


P3_NOTES = """# Phase 3 method notes

- **SEALED loader (reused, not rewritten):** `load_dev_window()` / `window_arrays()` / `Track` from the Phase 1 runner,
  with `WINDOW_DAY = 2017-11-08`, `DAY_START_ROW = 68,941,878`, `SKIP_MARGIN = 6,000,000` (Phase 0 SEALED window,
  08:00-16:00). Fold sizes: train 1,469,995 / early-stop 591,583 / eval 2,198,357 (eval positives 14,798).
- **Baseline guard:** raw-feature baseline measured once with `lab.full.fit([], 0)`; abort if it differs from the frozen
  0.9742099952759418 by more than 0.0002.
- **Real-feature replay:** for each of the 30 embedded runs, the accepted real specs are recomputed on SEALED in their
  original order and stacked on the 4 raw columns.
- **Three-seed re-measurement:** baseline and final AUC are each the mean of three fresh LightGBM fits at seeds
  100, 101, 102. Gap closed = 100 * (final - baseline) / (frozen SEALED ceiling - baseline). Results are cached by the
  hash of the ordered accepted specs, so identical stacks are fit once.
- **Matched-seed decoy delta:** for each embedded gate-tested decoy, the underlying spec is computed on the full SEALED
  window, permuted with `make_decoy` (seed 7,000,000 + 1000 * run_index + decoy_index), and added to that run's final
  accepted stack. Delta = mean over seeds 100/101/102 of AUC(stack + decoy) - AUC(stack) at the same seed.
- **Decoy reporting is descriptive and post hoc:** naive_pass = delta > 0; materiality_pass = delta >= the frozen
  materiality floor. For FDR arms "accepted" = materiality_pass; for naive arms "accepted" = naive_pass. These are checks
  against each run's FINAL stack, not the stack at proposal time.
- **LORD++ was NOT re-simulated** because the original trial-level FDR state is not embedded; any re-simulation would
  be fabricated.
- The SEALED baseline was measured in Phase 0 and again in the validation run.
- **Disclosure:** an earlier invocation of this script aborted at the first run with a KeyError (embedded accepted
  records are specs, not {'spec': ...} wrappers) before any feature AUC was computed on SEALED; only the baseline was
  observed. The fix is a bug fix with no change to frozen references, seeds, thresholds or evaluation logic.
- No Phase 3 feature results were inspected before the final run.
- Embedded `tokens` are cumulative counters across runs within an arm; `tokens_prompt`/`tokens_completion` are
  per-run deltas (run 1 taken as-is, assuming the counter started at 0), raw values kept in `*_cumulative` columns.
  Tokens and `n_dup_total` are null for R and L-naive (random / not logged) and written as NaN.
- R and L-naive never logged `decoys_proposed` / `decoys_screened`. They are DERIVED: proposed = the per-run-number decoy
  schedule (10, 15, 10, 16, 9; identical across the four arms that logged it), screened = proposed - gate-tested.
  Flagged in the `decoy_counts_source` column.
- An earlier skeleton invocation wrote a `started` lock on 2026-11-19 13:06 (local results/phase3) before failing; no
  SEALED AUC from any feature set was computed or inspected.
"""


def p3_synthetic_window(n=6000):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(0)
    t = (np.arange(n) * 10).astype(np.int32)
    W = {f"c{i}": rng.randint(0, m, n).astype(np.int32) for i, m in enumerate((40, 30, 20, 15, 10))}
    W.update(t=t, h=(t // 3600).astype(np.int32), y=(rng.rand(n) < 0.06).astype(np.uint8))
    return W


def p3_self_test():
    import tempfile
    global load_dev_window

    def _forbidden():
        raise RuntimeError("AF_TEST must not read train.csv / SEALED")
    load_dev_window = _forbidden
    try:
        p3_guard(0.9, 0.97, PHASE3_BASE_TOL)
        raise AssertionError("baseline guard did not fire")
    except RuntimeError:
        pass
    p3_guard(0.97421, 0.9742099952759418, PHASE3_BASE_TOL)

    s1 = {"op": "count", "keys": ["c0"]}
    s2 = {"op": "nunique", "keys": ["c0", "c1"], "target": "c2"}
    fake = {
        "A_1": dict(arm="R", run=1, accepted=[s1, s2], decoys=[dict(idx=3, spec=s1, dev_accepted=False)],
                    dev_baseline=0.97, dev_final_auc=0.975, model="", minutes=1.0, tokens=None, n_proposals=60,
                    n_full_tests=10, n_dup_total=None, decoys_proposed=0, decoys_screened=0, decoys_tested=1,
                    accepted_decoys=0),
        "B_1": dict(arm="L-naive", run=1, accepted=[s1], decoys=[dict(idx=5, spec=s2, dev_accepted=False)],
                    dev_baseline=0.97, dev_final_auc=0.974, model="m", minutes=2.0,
                    tokens={"prompt": 10, "completion": 5}, n_proposals=60, n_full_tests=9, n_dup_total=3,
                    decoys_proposed=12, decoys_screened=10, decoys_tested=1, accepted_decoys=0),
    }
    W = p3_synthetic_window()
    lab = Lab(W)
    work = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="phase3_selftest_"))
    ev = P3Evaluator(lab)
    phase3_write_json(work / "phase3_lock.json", dict(status="started", test=True))
    runs_df, dec_df, arms_df = p3_execute(ev, fake, work, expected=False, log_prefix="[TEST] ")
    for cols, df in ((RUN_COLS, runs_df), (DECOY_COLS, dec_df), (ARM_COLS, arms_df)):
        missing = [c for c in cols if c not in df.columns]
        assert not missing, f"missing columns {missing}"
    for f in ("phase3_runs.csv", "phase3_runs.json", "phase3_decoys.csv", "phase3_arms.csv", "phase3_arms.md",
              "phase3_summary.json", "phase3_lock.json", "PHASE3_METHOD_NOTES.md"):
        assert (work / f).exists(), f"missing output {f}"
    assert json.loads((work / "phase3_lock.json").read_text())["status"] == "complete"
    print("SMOKE_TEST_OK", work)


def phase3_main():
    if TEST:
        return p3_self_test()

    work = Path("/kaggle/working/phase3") if Path("/kaggle/working").exists() else Path("./phase3_results")
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lock_path = work / "phase3_lock.json"
    if lock_path.exists() and json.loads(lock_path.read_text()).get("status") == "complete":
        raise RuntimeError("phase3_lock.json status=complete: SEALED already evaluated.")

    # --- provenance asserts (fail loudly, before touching SEALED) -----------------------------------------------
    inputs_sha = p3_hash(EMBEDDED_INPUTS)
    runs = EMBEDDED_INPUTS["runs"]
    assert hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(EMBEDDED_INPUTS, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() == EXPECTED_INPUTS_SHA256, \
        "EMBEDDED_INPUTS changed since freeze"
    assert SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN == 0.9742099952759418 and SEALED_CEILING == 0.985440922359235
    assert len(runs) == 30
    assert sorted({r["arm"] for r in runs.values()}) == P3_ARMS
    for a in P3_ARMS:
        assert sum(r["arm"] == a for r in runs.values()) == 5, f"arm {a} does not have 5 runs"
    assert sum(len(r.get("decoys", [])) for r in runs.values()) == 29

    # --- SEALED window (exact Phase 0 parameters) ---------------------------------------------------------------
    global WINDOW_DAY, DAY_START_ROW
    WINDOW_DAY = "2017-11-08"
    DAY_START_ROW = 68_941_878
    W = get_window(Path("/tmp/af_phase3_cache"))
    lab = Lab(W)
    ev = P3Evaluator(lab)                       # measures the 3-seed raw baseline once
    measured = float(lab.full.fit([], 0)["auc"])
    print(f"SEALED measured baseline (seed 0): {measured:.7f} | frozen: {SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN:.7f} "
          f"| 3-seed mean: {ev.baseline_mean:.7f}", flush=True)
    p3_guard(measured, SEALED_BASELINE_FROZEN, PHASE3_BASE_TOL)
    phase3_write_json(lock_path, dict(status="started", embedded_inputs_sha256=inputs_sha,
                                      script_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                                      started_at_utc=p3_now(), runs_done=0, decoys_done=0))

    runs_df, dec_df, arms_df = p3_execute(ev, runs, work, expected=True)
    print("PHASE3_COMPLETE")
    print(arms_df.round(4).to_string(index=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    phase3_main()

===== code/af_phase3_analyze.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
AutoFeature Mini-Lab - analysis: 4 pre-registered figures + exploratory diagnostics (ID1).

Run from the repo root:  python code/af_phase3_analyze.py
Reads : results/phase3/phase3_runs.csv, phase3_decoys.csv, results/<arm>/trials_<arm>_<run>.jsonl
Writes: figures/fig1..fig4*.png            (pre-registered figures)
        figures/diag_*.png                 (EXPLORATORY, post hoc)
        results/diagnostics/*.csv|json     (EXPLORATORY, post hoc)
No SEALED data is touched; this only reads Phase 3 outputs and trial logs.
"""
import argparse, json, math
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.patches import FancyBboxPatch

ARMS = ["R", "R-naive", "L-naive", "L-FDR", "L-FDR-3B", "L-FDR-14B"]
LABEL = {"R": "Random + FDR", "R-naive": "Random + naive", "L-naive": "7B + naive",
         "L-FDR": "7B + FDR", "L-FDR-3B": "3B + FDR", "L-FDR-14B": "14B + FDR"}
COLOR = {"R": "#1b9e77", "R-naive": "#66c2a5", "L-naive": "#e08214",
         "L-FDR": "#d95f02", "L-FDR-3B": "#7570b3", "L-FDR-14B": "#e7298a"}
SCALING_ORDER = ["R", "L-FDR-3B", "L-FDR", "L-FDR-14B", "R-naive", "L-naive"]
MATERIALITY = 0.00021865718476891195


def canon(spec):
    return json.dumps(spec, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))


def entropy_bits(counts):
    n = sum(counts.values())
    return -sum(c / n * math.log2(c / n) for c in counts.values()) if n else float("nan")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pre-registered figures
def fig1(path):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 3.2))
    ax.axis("off")
    boxes = ["Proposer\n(random / Qwen)", "Proxy screen\n10% IP subsample\ngain >= 0.0005",
             "Full DEV test", "Gate\n(naive | LORD++ FDR)", "Stack"]
    x = 0.02
    for i, b in enumerate(boxes):
        ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch((x, 0.35), 0.15, 0.42, boxstyle="round,pad=0.01", fc="#e8eef7", ec="#335"))
        ax.text(x + 0.075, 0.56, b, ha="center", va="center", fontsize=9)
        if i < len(boxes) - 1:
            ax.annotate("", xy=(x + 0.185, 0.56), xytext=(x + 0.155, 0.56), arrowprops=dict(arrowstyle="->"))
        x += 0.19
    ax.add_patch(FancyBboxPatch((0.02, 0.04), 0.92, 0.17, boxstyle="round,pad=0.01", fc="#fdecea", ec="#a33"))
    ax.text(0.48, 0.125, "SEALED day (2017-11-08), opened once at the end   |   20% of proposals are permuted-real decoys",
            ha="center", va="center", fontsize=9)
    ax.set_xlim(0, 1); ax.set_ylim(0, 0.9)
    fig.savefig(path, dpi=170, bbox_inches="tight"); plt.close(fig)


def fig2(runs, decoys, path):
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(16, 4.6), gridspec_kw={"width_ratios": [1.1, 1.1, 1.0]})
    xs = np.arange(len(ARMS))
    # (a) decoys by stage
    st = [("decoys_proposed", "proposed"), ("decoys_screened", "screened out"),
          ("n_decoys_tested", "gate-tested"), ("n_decoys_accepted", "accepted")]
    g = runs.groupby("arm")[[c for c, _ in st]].sum().reindex(ARMS)
    w = 0.2
    for i, (c, lab) in enumerate(st):
        axs[0].bar(xs + (i - 1.5) * w, g[c], w, label=lab)
    axs[0].set_xticks(xs); axs[0].set_xticklabels([LABEL[a] for a in ARMS], rotation=30, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    axs[0].set_title("(a) Decoys by stage (sum of 5 runs)"); axs[0].set_ylim(0, max(float(g.max().max()), 1) * 1.3); axs[0].legend(fontsize=8, ncol=2, loc="upper right")
    # (b) DEV vs SEALED gap closed, per arm
    for i, a in enumerate(ARMS):
        r = runs[runs.arm == a]
        axs[1].scatter(np.full(len(r), i) - 0.12, r.dev_gap_closed_pct, s=18, color="#888", alpha=.7,
                       label="DEV" if i == 0 else None)
        axs[1].scatter(np.full(len(r), i) + 0.12, r.sealed_gap_closed_pct, s=18, color=COLOR[a],
                       label="SEALED" if i == 0 else None)
        axs[1].hlines(r.dev_gap_closed_pct.mean(), i - .3, i - .0, color="#444")
        axs[1].hlines(r.sealed_gap_closed_pct.mean(), i + .0, i + .3, color=COLOR[a])
    axs[1].set_xticks(xs); axs[1].set_xticklabels([LABEL[a] for a in ARMS], rotation=30, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    axs[1].set_ylabel("gap closed (%)"); axs[1].set_title("(b) DEV (grey) vs SEALED (colour), dot = run, bar = mean")
    # (c) decoy delta on SEALED
    if len(decoys):
        for i, a in enumerate(ARMS):
            d = decoys[decoys.arm == a].delta_auc
            if len(d):
                axs[2].scatter(np.full(len(d), i) + np.random.RandomState(i).uniform(-.12, .12, len(d)), d * 1e4,
                               color=COLOR[a], s=22, alpha=.85)
        axs[2].axhline(0, color="k", lw=.8)
        axs[2].axhline(MATERIALITY * 1e4, color="r", ls="--", lw=1, label="materiality floor")
        axs[2].legend(fontsize=8)
    axs[2].set_xticks(xs); axs[2].set_xticklabels([LABEL[a] for a in ARMS], rotation=30, ha="right", fontsize=8)
    axs[2].set_ylabel("delta AUC on SEALED (x1e-4)")
    axs[2].set_title("(c) 29 re-permuted decoys added to final stack")
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(path, dpi=170); plt.close(fig)


def fig3(runs, path):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 4.6))
    pos = {a: i + (0.6 if i >= 4 else 0) for i, a in enumerate(SCALING_ORDER)}
    for a in SCALING_ORDER:
        r = runs[runs.arm == a].sort_values("run")
        jit = np.random.RandomState(7).uniform(-.1, .1, len(r))
        ax.scatter(pos[a] + jit, r.sealed_gap_closed_pct, color=COLOR[a], s=36, alpha=.9, zorder=3)
        ax.hlines(r.sealed_gap_closed_pct.mean(), pos[a] - .28, pos[a] + .28, color="k", lw=1.6, zorder=4)
        ax.hlines(r.sealed_gap_closed_pct.median(), pos[a] - .18, pos[a] + .18, color="k", lw=1, ls=":", zorder=4)
        n_eff = r.final_set_hash.nunique()
        if n_eff < len(r):
            ax.text(pos[a], r.sealed_gap_closed_pct.min() - 4, f"{n_eff} distinct\noutcomes", ha="center", fontsize=8)
    ax.axvline(3.8, color="#bbb", lw=1)
    ax.set_xticks([pos[a] for a in SCALING_ORDER])
    ax.set_xticklabels([LABEL[a] for a in SCALING_ORDER], rotation=20, ha="right")
    ax.set_ylabel("SEALED gap closed (%)")
    ax.set_title("Proposer scaling (dot = run, solid bar = mean, dotted = median); naive arms right of the line")
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(path, dpi=170); plt.close(fig)


def fig4(runs, path):
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 4.8))
    for a in ARMS:
        r = runs[runs.arm == a]
        ax.scatter(r.minutes, r.sealed_gap_closed_pct, color=COLOR[a], s=26, alpha=.55)
        ax.scatter(r.minutes.mean(), r.sealed_gap_closed_pct.mean(), color=COLOR[a], s=120, edgecolor="k",
                   label=LABEL[a], zorder=4)
    ax.set_xlabel("minutes per run (proposal + evaluation wall-clock)")
    ax.set_ylabel("SEALED gap closed (%)")
    ax.set_title("Cost frontier (small = run, large = arm mean)")
    ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(path, dpi=170); plt.close(fig)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ exploratory diagnostics (ID1)
def load_trials(root, arm, run):
    f = root / "results" / arm / f"trials_{arm}_{run}.jsonl"
    if not f.exists():
        return []
    return [json.loads(l) for l in open(f) if l.strip()]


def find_needle(root, runs, override=None):
    """Largest-gain accepted real feature (by d_full) in the best SEALED run."""
    if override:
        return json.load(open(override))
    best = runs.sort_values("sealed_gap_closed_pct", ascending=False).iloc[0]
    rows = [r for r in load_trials(root, best.arm, int(best.run))
            if r.get("status") == "accepted" and not r.get("is_decoy") and r.get("d_full") is not None
            and r.get("spec") is not None]
    if not rows:
        return None
    top = max(rows, key=lambda r: r["d_full"])
    return dict(arm=best.arm, run=int(best.run), sealed_gap_closed_pct=float(best.sealed_gap_closed_pct),
                spec=top["spec"], d_full=top["d_full"], i=top["i"], canon=canon(top["spec"]))


def diagnostics(root, runs, outdir, figdir):
    outdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    needle = find_needle(root, runs)
    rows, curves = [], {}
    for _, rr in runs.iterrows():
        arm, run = rr.arm, int(rr.run)
        all_rows = [t for t in load_trials(root, arm, run) if not t.get("is_decoy")]
        tr = [t for t in all_rows if t.get("spec") is not None]      # rows without a spec (invalid etc.) are counted below
        if not all_rows:
            continue
        status_counts = Counter(t.get("status") for t in all_rows)
        specs = [canon(t["spec"]) for t in tr if t.get("spec") is not None]
        ops = Counter(t["spec"].get("op") for t in tr if t.get("spec") is not None)
        acc = [t for t in tr if t.get("status") == "accepted"]
        nd_i = next((t["i"] for t in tr if needle and canon(t["spec"]) == needle["canon"]), np.nan)
        nd_acc = bool(needle and any(canon(t["spec"]) == needle["canon"] for t in acc))
        rows.append(dict(arm=arm, run=run, n_real_logged=len(tr), unique_specs=len(set(specs)),
                         repeat_frac=1 - len(set(specs)) / max(len(specs), 1),
                         op_entropy_bits=entropy_bits(ops), n_ops_used=len(ops),
                         n_rows_without_spec=len(all_rows) - len(tr),
                         status_counts=json.dumps(dict(status_counts), sort_keys=True),
                         n_accepted=len(acc), first_accept_i=min((t["i"] for t in acc), default=np.nan),
                         last_accept_i=max((t["i"] for t in acc), default=np.nan),
                         best_d_full=max((t["d_full"] for t in acc), default=np.nan),
                         needle_first_proposed_i=nd_i, needle_ever_proposed=not np.isnan(nd_i),
                         needle_accepted=nd_acc, n_dup_logged=sum(t.get("n_dup", 0) or 0 for t in tr),
                         sealed_gap_closed_pct=rr.sealed_gap_closed_pct))
        order = sorted(tr, key=lambda t: t["i"])
        curves[(arm, run)] = np.cumsum([1 if t.get("status") == "accepted" else 0 for t in order]), \
            [t["i"] for t in order]
    d = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    d.to_csv(outdir / "diag_by_run.csv", index=False)
    arm = d.groupby("arm").agg(
        runs=("run", "count"), unique_specs_mean=("unique_specs", "mean"), repeat_frac_mean=("repeat_frac", "mean"),
        op_entropy_mean=("op_entropy_bits", "mean"), accepted_mean=("n_accepted", "mean"),
        first_accept_i_median=("first_accept_i", "median"), best_d_full_mean=("best_d_full", "mean"),
        needle_ever_proposed=("needle_ever_proposed", "sum"), needle_accepted=("needle_accepted", "sum")
    ).reindex(ARMS)
    arm.to_csv(outdir / "diag_by_arm.csv")
    if needle:
        json.dump({k: v for k, v in needle.items() if k != "canon"}, open(outdir / "needle.json", "w"), indent=2)

    # E1: diversity
    fig, axs = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(15, 4.4))
    for ax, col, ttl in zip(axs, ["unique_specs", "op_entropy_bits", "needle_first_proposed_i"],
                            ["Unique feature specs proposed (of ~60)", "Operation entropy (bits, max 2.58)",
                             "Proposal index where the needle was first proposed"]):
        for i, a in enumerate(ARMS):
            v = d[d.arm == a][col]
            ax.scatter(np.full(len(v), i) + np.random.RandomState(i).uniform(-.1, .1, len(v)), v,
                       color=COLOR[a], s=30, alpha=.85)
            if v.notna().any():
                ax.hlines(v.mean(), i - .25, i + .25, color="k")
        ax.set_xticks(range(len(ARMS))); ax.set_xticklabels([LABEL[a] for a in ARMS], rotation=30, ha="right", fontsize=8)
        ax.set_title(ttl, fontsize=10)
    axs[2].text(0.02, 0.02, "missing dot = needle never proposed", transform=axs[2].transAxes, fontsize=8)
    fig.suptitle("EXPLORATORY (post hoc): proposal diversity and the 'needle' feature", y=1.02, fontsize=11)
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(figdir / "diag_diversity_needle.png", dpi=170, bbox_inches="tight"); plt.close(fig)

    # E2: discovery curves (cumulative accepted real features vs proposal index)
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(7.5, 4.6))
    for a in ARMS:
        ys = []
        for (ar, run), (cum, idx) in curves.items():
            if ar != a:
                continue
            y = np.zeros(61); last = 0
            for c, i in zip(cum, idx):
                y[min(int(i), 60):] = c
            ys.append(y)
        if ys:
            ax.plot(range(61), np.mean(ys, axis=0), color=COLOR[a], label=LABEL[a], lw=2)
    ax.set_xlabel("proposal index"); ax.set_ylabel("accepted real features (mean of 5 runs)")
    ax.set_title("EXPLORATORY: discovery curves", fontsize=10); ax.legend(fontsize=8)
    fig.tight_layout(); fig.savefig(figdir / "diag_discovery_curves.png", dpi=170); plt.close(fig)
    return d, arm, needle


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=".")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    root = Path(a.root).resolve()
    figdir = root / "figures"; figdir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    runs = pd.read_csv(root / "results" / "phase3" / "phase3_runs.csv")
    dec_f = root / "results" / "phase3" / "phase3_decoys.csv"
    decoys = pd.read_csv(dec_f) if dec_f.exists() else pd.DataFrame()
    assert len(runs) == 30, f"expected 30 runs, got {len(runs)}"
    fig1(figdir / "fig1_design_diagram.png")
    fig2(runs, decoys, figdir / "fig2_noise_kept_and_sealed_drop.png")
    fig3(runs, figdir / "fig3_proposer_scaling.png")
    fig4(runs, figdir / "fig4_cost_frontier.png")
    print("pre-registered figures written to", figdir)
    d, arm, needle = diagnostics(root, runs, root / "results" / "diagnostics", figdir)
    print("\nNEEDLE (largest-gain accepted feature in best SEALED run):",
          None if not needle else {k: v for k, v in needle.items() if k != "canon"})
    print("\nDIAGNOSTICS BY ARM (exploratory, post hoc)\n", arm.round(2).to_string())
    tot = Counter()
    for sc in d.status_counts:
        tot.update(json.loads(sc))
    print("\nSTATUS VOCABULARY (all non-decoy logged rows):", dict(tot))
    print("rows without spec per arm:", d.groupby("arm").n_rows_without_spec.sum().reindex(ARMS).to_dict())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
